      "title": "Streamline User Journeys with Verified Email via Credential Manager",

      "summary": "Posted by Niharika Arora, Senior Developer Relations Engineer and Jean-Pierre Pralle, Product Manager, Credential Manager In the modern digital landscape, the first encounter a user has with an app is often the most critical.
